Pro Tour Philadelphia featured the debut of the Modern Constructed format. Listed below are players with last names starting with A-E who did not record 18 points or more during the 10 rounds of Modern.

For more deck lists, check out these links:

*   [Top 8 Decks](https://web.archive.org/web/20110904000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/eventcoverage/ptphi11/top8decklists)
*   [Modern Deck Lists, 18 Points or Better](https://web.archive.org/web/20110904000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/eventcoverage/ptphi11/topmoderndecks)
*   Other Modern Deck Lists: [A-E](https://web.archive.org/web/20110904000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/eventcoverage/ptphi11/decksae) | [F-L](https://web.archive.org/web/20110904000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/eventcoverage/ptphi11/decksfl) | [M-R](https://web.archive.org/web/20110904000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/eventcoverage/ptphi11/decksmr) | [S-Z](https://web.archive.org/web/20110904000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/eventcoverage/ptphi11/deckssz)
